- **si-competence-floor**: In domains where you are weak, you are least able to detect that you are weak — the skills needed to do the task are the same skills needed to judge it. In unfamiliar territory, distrust the "looks right" signal and verify externally. Also corrects the expert's mirror error: assuming the user knows what you know. Use on unfamiliar stacks, languages, or domains. ([jpoindexter/si-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/si-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-competence-floor) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **si-confidence-build**: Provide evidence-based exercises to build confidence. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-confidence-build))
- **si-confidence-source**: Audit WHY something feels right before trusting the feeling. Three false cues — fluency (easy to generate), pseudorelevance (knowing a lot around the topic), and inherited self-view ("I'm good at this") — produce confidence unconnected to accuracy. Name the cue; if it's one of these, downgrade and verify. Use whenever a confident answer arrives without an explicit check. ([jpoindexter/si-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/si-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-confidence-source) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **si-construe-the-situation**: Most prediction errors are misread situations, not unknown selves. Before claiming "this will work," get the actual situation — read the real file, check the real env/config/versions, look at the real data shape and the error paths — instead of predicting against a clean, abstract, happy-path sketch. Lower confidence in proportion to how much of the real situation you haven't seen. Use before predicting any runtime behavior, claiming code will work, or estimating an outcome. ([jpoindexter/si-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/si-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-construe-the-situation) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **si-corporate-income-tax**: Slovenian corporate income tax (davek od dohodkov pravnih oseb, DDPO) for tax year 2026. Covers who pays (resident companies on worldwide profit, non-residents through a Slovenian PE), the 22% rate for 2024 to 2028 under ZORZFS art. 64 (the ZDDPO-2 art. 60 rate is 19%), the taxable base and non-deductible items, the 50% rules for entertainment and provisions, maximum depreciation rates, the R&D (100%), investment (40%) and digital and green (40%) reliefs, losses (five years, 50% of the base) and the 63% overall cap in art. 59.a, the interest limitation rule (30% of EBITDA or EUR 3 million), a… ([openaccountants/openaccountants](https://github.com/openaccountants/openaccountants/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/si-corporate-income-tax) · ★498)
- **si-corrective-feedback**: Feedback is the riskiest thing you do — nearly 40% of feedback interventions make performance WORSE. The difference is altitude: criticism aimed at the task and the next concrete step helps; criticism aimed at the author's worth triggers self-protection and backfires. Attack the code, name the fix, frame the gap as closable. Use when you critique code, review a sub-agent's output, or push back on a user's plan. ([jpoindexter/si-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/si-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-corrective-feedback) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **si-cross-border**: Hub skill for crossing the Slovenian border in tax year 2026. - **si-define-the-criterion**: Vague success words — done, clean, secure, fast, good, robust — get defined self-servingly to flatter whatever you produced. Pin the criterion to concrete, observable, falsifiable terms drawn from the task, BEFORE doing the work, so the bar can't move to meet the output. Use whenever success is stated in an ambiguous trait word. ([jpoindexter/si-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/si-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-define-the-criterion) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **si-docs**: 프로그램 목록·테이블 정의서·화면 기능 TC 결과서를 소스와 실행 근거로 작성한다. '프로그램 목록 엑셀 만들어 줘', 'DDL로 테이블 정의서 작성해 줘', '화면 단위테스트 결과서 만들어 줘', '우리 양식에 결과 채워 줘' 같은 SI 산출물 요청에 사용한다. ([MycroCosmo/sistack](https://github.com/MycroCosmo/sistack/tree/HEAD/skills/si-docs))
- **si-duan-kuo-chong**: 何时调用：当用户需要将内在的善端（恻隐、羞恶、恭敬、是非）有意识地培养壮大时。 关键信号："怎么培养善心？"、"善端怎么壮大？"、"怎么让道德成长？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是理解性善论（用 xing-shan-lun），或用户需要的是浩然之气（用 hao-ran-zhi-qi）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/meng-zi/si-duan-kuo-chong) · ★1)
- **si-egocentric-check**: You overweight your own frame and forget the other party — the user, another system, the competing process — operates under the same constraints you do. Before any comparative, "this is better/unique/special," or controllability claim, explicitly model the other side. Use on relative judgments, uniqueness claims, and estimates of how much you control an outcome. ([jpoindexter/si-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/si-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-egocentric-check) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **si-el-lo-hace-tu-tambien**: Use before giving your own technical justification when it rests mainly on popularity, trend, convention, or what another codebase does rather than on the requirements at hand. Name the requirement and the trade-off instead. NOT when the user already chose the tool; use porque-yo-lo-digo. ([mcasillas17/mexican-mom](https://github.com/mcasillas17/mexican-mom/tree/HEAD/skills/si-el-lo-hace-tu-tambien) · ★2)

- **si-feedback-integrity**: A passing signal rarely proves what it seems to. Feedback is probabilistic, incomplete, hidden, ambiguous, absent, or biased — and the default habit is to accept what flatters and explain away what doesn't. Before treating a green test, a "looks good," or a success as proof, ask what it does NOT establish, and seek the disconfirming signal. Use when a result is about to be read as validation. ([jpoindexter/si-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/si-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-feedback-integrity) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **si-improve**: Repair a recurring, evidenced convention or documentation gap found during work in Codex. Use when missing, wrong, or ambiguous guidance caused a concrete problem; exclude one-off code mistakes, initial setup, and archive-only cleanup. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/plugins/self-improvement/skills/si-improve) · ★1)
- **si-improve**: Use when a convention, document, or procedure was missing, wrong, or ambiguous and it cost you time during the work, and that gap looks likely to recur in other files, features, or tasks. Also use when the end-of-work check ("of what bit me, what will recur?") surfaces a gap, and on requests like "self-improvement", "fix the convention", "we have a convention gap". Do NOT load for: one-off mistakes, fixing the code bug itself, trimming bloated convention docs (si-archive owns that), or first-time setup in a project without the system (si-init owns that). ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/skills/si-improve) · ★1)
- **si-init**: Bootstrap or register a project's convention repair system in Codex, preserving existing records and separating Codex instruction wiring from Claude. Use for initial setup or checking the setup after a plugin update. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/plugins/self-improvement/skills/si-init) · ★1)
- **si-init**: Use to bootstrap a project once, when it has no self-improvement data file (convention changelog and routing table). Triggers include "set up self-improvement", "si-init", and si-improve / si-archive / convention-smith failing to find the data file. Also re-run it right after a plugin update — it compares the data file's version stamp and repairs stale template wording (project data is never touched). Re-running is safe and idempotent: when it detects an existing system it registers and repairs rather than overwriting. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/skills/si-init) · ★1)

- **si-ju-jiao**: 何时调用：当用户需要一套完整的日常修行框架，或在"修心"过程中需要具体的操作步骤时。 关键信号："怎么修心？""日常怎么练？""心学具体怎么操作？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是哲学基础（用 xin-ji-li），或用户需要的是知行合一（用 zhi-xing-he-yi）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/yang-ming-xin-xue/si-ju-jiao) · ★1)
